{"parentUuid":"de14b468-f815-4367-b808-23a2a518dc9b","isSidechain":false,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\User\\comfyui-agent","sessionId":"67e75800-e1e5-454a-a526-125e3ebaa9f6","version":"2.1.51","gitBranch":"master","slug":"smooth-brewing-grove","type":"progress","data":{"message":{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015n17enmALaRNRy5iuwa5WE","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# ComfyUI Agent — Response Design System\n     2→\n     3→> **For:** Claude Code agent teams building ComfyUI co-pilot interfaces\n     4→> **Principle:** The agent speaks ComfyUI's visual language. Every response should feel like it was designed by Pentagram as a native panel, not generated by an LLM.\n     5→\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→## 1. Core Philosophy\n     9→\n    10→**The artist doesn't want to read. They want to glance.**\n    11→\n    12→Every response must pass the **3-Second Test**: Can the artist understand what happened, what matters, and what to do next within 3 seconds of looking at the panel?\n    13→\n    14→Three rules, never broken:\n    15→\n    16→1. **Summary-first.** The first thing visible answers \"what is this\" in one line.\n    17→2. **Progressive disclosure.** Show the minimum. Let them drill. Collapsed by default.\n    18→3. **Native vocabulary.** Use ComfyUI's own colors, terms, and spatial patterns. No emoji. No markdown headers. No bullet walls.\n    19→\n    20→---\n    21→\n    22→## 2. Brand & Color System\n    23→\n    24→### 2.1 Brand Colors\n    25→\n    26→```\n    27→BRAND PRIMARY    #00BB81   Jade green — accent, interactive, \"ready\" state\n    28→BRAND DIM        #00BB8133 Low-opacity brand for subtle backgrounds\n    29→BRAND SUBTLE     #00BB8118 Ultra-low-opacity for section highlights\n    30→DARK             #020410   Black Pearl — deepest background\n    31→```\n    32→\n    33→### 2.2 Surface Hierarchy\n    34→\n    35→Build depth through layered surfaces, not borders or shadows.\n    36→\n    37→```\n    38→canvas           #0d0f14   Page / app background\n    39→panel            #141620   Panel container\n    40→card             #1a1d2a   Card within panel\n    41→card-hover       #1e2133   Hover state\n    42→elevated         #222538   Popovers, tooltips\n    43→input            #12141e   Input fields\n    44→```\n    45→\n    46→### 2.3 Text Hierarchy\n    47→\n    48→Four levels. Never more. If you need a fifth, you've over-structured.\n    49→\n    50→```\n    51→primary          #e8e9ed   Titles, key values, names\n    52→secondary        #9a9caa   Descriptions, body text\n    53→muted            #5c5e6e   Labels, timestamps, meta\n    54→accent           #c0c2ce   Emphasis within body text\n    55→brand-text       #00BB81   Interactive labels, status\n    56→```\n    57→\n    58→### 2.4 Node Slot Colors (Official ComfyUI Dark Theme)\n    59→\n    60→These are the artist's existing color vocabulary. Use them semantically.\n    61→\n    62→```\n    63→CLIP             #FFD500   Text encoding\n    64→CLIP_VISION      #A8DADC   Vision encoding\n    65→CONDITIONING     #FFA931   Prompt conditioning\n    66→CONTROL_NET      #6EE7B7   ControlNet\n    67→IMAGE            #64B5F6   Image data\n    68→LATENT           #FF9CF9   Latent space\n    69→MASK             #81C784   Masks\n    70→MODEL            #B39DDB   Model/checkpoint\n    71→STYLE_MODEL      #C2FFAE   Style transfer\n    72→VAE              #FF6E6E   VAE encode/decode\n    73→NOISE            #B0B0B0   Noise/randomness\n    74→GUIDER           #66FFFF   Guidance/CFG\n    75→SAMPLER          #ECB4B4   Sampling\n    76→SIGMAS           #CDFFCD   Noise schedule\n    77→```\n    78→\n    79→### 2.5 Semantic Mapping\n    80→\n    81→Map node function → slot color. When displaying a node or concept, use the color of its data type, not an arbitrary palette.\n    82→\n    83→```\n    84→\"loaded a model\"        → MODEL (#B39DDB)\n    85→\"encoded your prompt\"   → CLIP (#FFD500) or CONDITIONING (#FFA931)\n    86→\"sampling\"              → SAMPLER (#ECB4B4)\n    87→\"decoded latents\"       → VAE (#FF6E6E)\n    88→\"final image/video\"     → IMAGE (#64B5F6)\n    89→\"guidance settings\"     → GUIDER (#66FFFF)\n    90→\"noise schedule\"        → SIGMAS (#CDFFCD)\n    91→```\n    92→\n    93→---\n    94→\n    95→## 3. Typography\n    96→\n    97→### 3.1 Font Stack\n    98→\n    99→```\n   100→sans    \"Inter\", \"SF Pro Display\", system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif\n   101→mono    \"JetBrains Mono\", \"Fira Code\", \"SF Mono\", \"Cascadia Code\", monospace\n   102→```\n   103→\n   104→### 3.2 Type Scale\n   105→\n   106→```\n   107→Panel title          16px  sans  600  -0.02em tracking\n   108→Section header       12px  sans  500   0.01em\n   109→Body text            12px  sans  400   normal\n   110→Label (uppercase)    10px  mono  400   0.06-0.08em  uppercase\n   111→Tag / code           11px  mono  400   0.01em\n   112→Detail value         10.5px mono 400   0.01em\n   113→Stat number          14px  mono  600   normal\n   114→```\n   115→\n   116→### 3.3 Rules\n   117→\n   118→- **Mono** for: node names, values, counts, code, tags, status labels\n   119→- **Sans** for: descriptions, section titles, body text, button labels\n   120→- **Never mix** in the same line. A line is mono or sans, not both.\n   121→- Exception: A sans-serif label followed by a mono value on the same row (detail rows)\n   122→\n   123→---\n   124→\n   125→## 4. Spacing System\n   126→\n   127→Breathing room is content. Cramped panels feel like error messages.\n   128→\n   129→### 4.1 Base Unit: 4px\n   130→\n   131→```\n   132→Micro gap         4px    Between dot and label\n   133→Small gap         6px    Between tags in a row\n   134→Medium gap        8-10px Between items in a list\n   135→Section padding   14-16px Horizontal panel padding\n   136→Section gap       12px   Between content blocks within a section\n   137→Header padding    16px top, 14px bottom\n   138→Footer padding    10px vertical\n   139→```\n   140→\n   141→### 4.2 Vertical Rhythm\n   142→\n   143→Every section separator is a 1px line at `border-subtle` opacity. No heavy borders. No double-lines. The separation comes from background color shifts and spacing, not from drawn lines.\n   144→\n   145→```\n   146→Between sections     1px solid #1f2230 (border-subtle)\n   147→Within sections      12px gap (no line)\n   148→After section title  6-8px before content\n   149→```\n   150→\n   151→---\n   152→\n   153→## 5. Component Library\n   154→\n   155→### 5.1 Panel Header (always visible)\n   156→\n   157→This is the only thing the artist MUST read. Everything else is optional.\n   158→\n   159→```\n   160→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   161→│ workflow · analysis            [video gen]   │  ← label (mono, muted, uppercase) + type badge\n   162→│                                             │\n   163→│ LTXV Video — Tiled + Nemotron               │  ← title (sans, 16px, 600)\n   164→│                                             │\n   165→│ Dual-path video generation with             │  ← summary (sans, 12px, secondary)\n   166→│ deterministic optimization. Tiled           │     MAX 2 sentences. This IS the answer.\n   167→│ sampling enables high-res beyond VRAM.      │\n   168→│                                             │\n   169→│ ~15 nodes    2 paths    video output        │  ← stat bar (mono numbers + sans labels)\n   170→└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n   171→```\n   172→\n   173→**Rules:**\n   174→- Type badge uses brand color background at 18% opacity\n   175→- Summary is 1-2 sentences. If it takes 3, you're over-explaining.\n   176→- Stat bar: max 3-4 stats. Number in mono (14px, 600), label in sans (10px, muted)\n   177→\n   178→### 5.2 Collapsible Section\n   179→\n   180→Default state: **collapsed**. Only open \"Signal Flow\" and \"Capabilities\" by default.\n   181→\n   182→```\n   183→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   184→│ ● Models & Loaders                     3  ▸ │  ← dot (slot color) + title + count + chevron\n   185→├─────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n   186→│   [CheckpointLoader] [CLIPLoader] [Nem..]   │  ← slot tags (colored by type)\n   187→│                                             │\n   188→│   Optimization ··················· Determ.   │  ← detail rows (label + dots + value)\n   189→│   VAE noise ····················· Configured │\n   190→└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n   191→```\n   192→\n   193→**Rules:**\n   194→- Section dot color = primary slot color for that section\n   195→- Chevron rotates 90° on open (CSS transition, 150ms)\n   196→- Open sections get a subtle background tint: `${slotColor}06`\n   197→- Count badge is mono, muted, right-aligned\n   198→\n   199→### 5.3 Slot Tag\n   200→\n   201→A node or concept, displayed as a colored chip.\n   202→\n   203→```\n   204→● CLIPTextEncode ×2\n   205→```\n   206→\n   207→- Dot (slot color, 5px, with glow shadow at 55% opacity)\n   208→- Label (mono, 11px, slot color text)\n   209→- Background: slot color at 12% opacity\n   210→- Border: slot color at 25% opacity\n   211→- Border-radius: 3px\n   212→- Padding: 2px 8px 2px 6px\n   213→\n   214→### 5.4 Flow Chain\n   215→\n   216→Shows signal path through nodes. This replaces bullet-point descriptions of pipelines.\n   217→\n   218→```\n   219→Checkpoint → CLIP → BaseSampler → VAEDecode → Combine\n   220→```\n   221→\n   222→- Each node name in mono, 11px, colored by its slot type\n   223→- Node has a subtle background tint: `${color}0a`\n   224→- Arrow is an SVG path, muted color, 16px wide\n   225→- Wrap allowed (flex-wrap) — long chains break to next line gracefully\n   226→- Path label prefix (e.g., \"base path\") in mono, 10px, muted, uppercase\n   227→\n   228→### 5.5 Detail Row\n   229→\n   230→Key-value pair with dotted leader. For specifications, parameters, metadata.\n   231→\n   232→```\n   233→Optimization ··················· Deterministic\n   234→CFG ···························· Dynamic scaling\n   235→```\n   236→\n   237→- Label: sans, 11px, muted, left-aligned\n   238→- Leader: dotted bottom-border on a flex-expanded div\n   239→- Value: mono, 10.5px, secondary, right-aligned\n   240→- Padding: 4px vertical per row\n   241→\n   242→### 5.6 Capability Row\n   243→\n   244→A feature with a brief explanation. Reads like a spec sheet.\n   245→\n   246→```\n   247→● Deterministic ──────────── Same seed → same output\n   248→● High-res tiling ─────────── Beyond VRAM limits\n   249→```\n   250→\n   251→- Dot (brand color or relevant slot color, 4px)\n   252→- Label: sans, 11px, accent, 500 weight\n   253→- Separator: 1px line, border-subtle\n   254→- Description: sans, 10.5px, muted\n   255→\n   256→### 5.7 Action Footer\n   257→\n   258→What the artist can do next. Always at the bottom.\n   259→\n   260→```\n   261→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   262→│ ready                    [Modify]  [▊ Run]  │\n   263→└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n   264→```\n   265→\n   266→- Status label: mono, 10px, muted\n   267→- Secondary button: transparent bg, border, secondary text\n   268→- Primary button: brand green bg, dark text\n   269→- Border-radius: 4px, padding: 5px 14px\n   270→\n   271→---\n   272→\n   273→## 6. Response Templates\n   274→\n   275→### 6.1 Workflow Analysis (inspect/understand)\n   276→\n   277→```\n   278→HEADER\n   279→  label: \"workflow · analysis\"\n   280→  badge: workflow type (e.g., \"video gen\", \"img2img\", \"inpainting\")\n   281→  title: Model name + key technique\n   282→  summary: 1-2 sentences. What it does and what makes it notable.\n   283→  stats: node count, path count, output type\n   284→\n   285→SECTIONS (collapsed by default)\n   286→  Signal Flow (open): flow chains showing data paths\n   287→  [One section per node category]: slot tags + detail rows\n   288→  Capabilities (open): capability rows\n   289→\n   290→FOOTER\n   291→  status + action buttons\n   292→```\n   293→\n   294→### 6.2 Modification Confirmation (pilot/modify)\n   295→\n   296→```\n   297→HEADER\n   298→  label: \"workflow · modified\"\n   299→  badge: change type (e.g., \"param\", \"node swap\", \"restructure\")\n   300→  title: What changed, in plain English\n   301→  summary: \"Changed X from A to B\" — concrete, verifiable\n   302→\n   303→SECTIONS\n   304→  Changes (open): before → after detail rows\n   305→  Affected nodes: slot tags of touched nodes\n   306→  Unchanged: brief note of what wasn't affected\n   307→\n   308→FOOTER\n   309→  status: \"modified · not yet executed\" + [Undo] [Run]\n   310→```\n   311→\n   312→### 6.3 Execution Result (execute/verify)\n   313→\n   314→```\n   315→HEADER\n   316→  label: \"execution · complete\"\n   317→  badge: \"success\" (green) or \"failed\" (VAE red)\n   318→  title: What was produced\n   319→  summary: Generation time, output dimensions, seed\n   320→\n   321→SECTIONS\n   322→  Output: image/video preview if available\n   323→  Parameters used: detail rows of key settings\n   324→  Quality notes: any observations from vision analysis\n   325→\n   326→FOOTER\n   327→  status + [Iterate] [Save] [New prompt]\n   328→```\n   329→\n   330→### 6.4 Discovery Results (search/discover)\n   331→\n   332→```\n   333→HEADER\n   334→  label: \"discovery · models\" or \"discovery · nodes\"\n   335→  badge: result count\n   336→  title: What was searched\n   337→  summary: Top result + why it's relevant\n   338→\n   339→SECTIONS\n   340→  Results: one sub-section per result with name, description, compatibility\n   341→  Related: cross-references to installed vs available\n   342→\n   343→FOOTER\n   344→  status + [Install] [Details]\n   345→```\n   346→\n   347→### 6.5 Error / Issue\n   348→\n   349→```\n   350→HEADER\n   351→  label: \"issue · detected\"\n   352→  badge: severity (\"warning\" in CONDITIONING orange, \"error\" in VAE red)\n   353→  title: What went wrong, plain English\n   354→  summary: Most likely cause in one sentence\n   355→\n   356→SECTIONS\n   357→  Details (open): the technical specifics\n   358→  Suggestions: numbered recovery steps (max 3)\n   359→  Context: what was happening when it failed\n   360→\n   361→FOOTER\n   362→  status + [Retry] [Skip]\n   363→```\n   364→\n   365→---\n   366→\n   367→## 7. Response Formatting Rules\n   368→\n   369→These apply to ALL agent output, not just panels.\n   370→\n   371→### 7.1 Text Responses (non-panel conversational replies)\n   372→\n   373→When the agent responds in plain text (not a structured panel), follow these rules:\n   374→\n   375→**Spacing:**\n   376→- One blank line between paragraphs. Always.\n   377→- Two blank lines before a new topic/section.\n   378→- Never more than 3 sentences in a row without a visual break.\n   379→\n   380→**Structure:**\n   381→- Lead with the answer. First sentence = what the artist needs to know.\n   382→- Supporting detail follows. Never bury the answer.\n   383→- If listing things, use inline lists (\"X, Y, and Z\") not bullet points — unless there are 5+ items or each needs explanation.\n   384→\n   385→**Tone:**\n   386→- Terse over verbose. \"Changed steps from 20 to 30\" not \"I've gone ahead and modified the number of sampling steps from the previous value of 20 to a new value of 30.\"\n   387→- Technical over casual. Artists respect precision. Don't dumb it down.\n   388→- Active voice. \"Loaded SDXL checkpoint\" not \"The SDXL checkpoint has been loaded.\"\n   389→- No filler phrases: \"I'd be happy to\", \"Let me help you with\", \"Great question!\"\n   390→\n   391→**Node references in text:**\n   392→- Always use the exact node class name, not a paraphrase\n   393→- Format in monospace/code style when the rendering supports it\n   394→- Color the reference by slot type when possible\n   395→\n   396→### 7.2 Length Guidelines\n   397→\n   398→```\n   399→Quick confirmation      1-2 sentences, no panel\n   400→Parameter change        Panel header only (no sections needed)\n   401→Workflow analysis       Full panel with sections\n   402→Error diagnosis         Panel with details + suggestions open\n   403→Discovery results       Panel with results, max 5 shown\n   404→```\n   405→\n   406→### 7.3 The \"Don't Make Me Read\" Principle\n   407→\n   408→Before sending any response, apply this filter:\n   409→\n   410→1. Can I cut this in half? → Do it.\n   411→2. Is anything repeated? → Remove it.\n   412→3. Is the first sentence the answer? → If not, restructure.\n   413→4. Would a section heading serve the artist better than prose? → Use the panel.\n   414→5. Am I explaining something the artist already knows? → Remove it.\n   415→\n   416→---\n   417→\n   418→## 8. Anti-Patterns (Never Do This)\n   419→\n   420→| Pattern | Why It Fails | Do Instead |\n   421→|---------|-------------|------------|\n   422→| Emoji as headers (🎯 📝 🧠) | Childish, not scannable, not native | Colored dots using slot colors |\n   423→| Markdown bullet walls | Equal visual weight, no hierarchy | Collapsible sections with slot tags |\n   424→| \"What This Workflow Excels At\" | Sales copy, not a tool | \"Capabilities\" spec sheet |\n   425→| Bold **every** node name | Visual noise, nothing stands out | Colored mono-font tags |\n   426→| \"Would you like me to...\" at the end | Passive, wastes space | Action buttons in footer |\n   427→| Restating the question | Artist knows what they asked | Jump to the answer |\n   428→| H2/H3 markdown headers | Flat hierarchy, all equally loud | Progressive disclosure with collapse |\n   429→| \"This is a sophisticated workflow\" | Subjective filler | Concrete: \"Dual-path with tiling\" |\n   430→| Long paragraphs of explanation | Nobody reads paragraph 3 | Summary sentence + collapsible detail |\n   431→| Numbered lists for features | Implies sequence where none exists | Capability rows or inline text |\n   432→\n   433→---\n   434→\n   435→## 9. Implementation Notes\n   436→\n   437→### 9.1 For HTML/React Panel Rendering\n   438→\n   439→- All colors as CSS custom properties for theme switching\n   440→- Transitions: 150ms ease for collapse/expand, hover states\n   441→- Scrollbar: 3px width, track transparent, thumb at border color\n   442→- Font loading: Import Inter + JetBrains Mono from Google Fonts\n   443→- Max panel width: 500px (sidebar constraint)\n   444→- Overflow: scrollY with thin custom scrollbar\n   445→\n   446→### 9.2 For Plain Text Fallback\n   447→\n   448→When the agent can't render a panel (CLI, plain text contexts):\n   449→\n   450→```\n   451→─ LTXV Video — Tiled + Nemotron ────────────────\n   452→  video gen · ~15 nodes · 2 paths\n   453→\n   454→  Dual-path video generation with deterministic\n   455→  optimization. Tiled sampling for high-res.\n   456→\n   457→  Flow: Checkpoint → CLIP → Sampler → VAE → Combine\n   458→  Tiled: TiledSampler → VAEDecode → Resize → Combine\n   459→  Merge: AdaIN Blend\n   460→\n   461→  ✓ Deterministic    ✓ High-res tiling\n   462→  ✓ Dual-path blend  ✓ STG guidance\n   463→\n   464→  [Modify]  [Run]\n   465→────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   466→```\n   467→\n   468→Use box-drawing characters, not markdown. Align with monospace assumptions. Keep the same information hierarchy: title → summary → flow → capabilities → actions.\n   469→\n   470→### 9.3 For Markdown Contexts (where panels aren't possible)\n   471→\n   472→When stuck in markdown-only rendering:\n   473→\n   474→```markdown\n   475→**LTXV Video — Tiled + Nemotron** · video gen\n   476→\n   477→Dual-path video generation with deterministic optimization.\n   478→Tiled sampling enables high-res output beyond VRAM limits.\n   479→\n   480→`Checkpoint → CLIP → BaseSampler → VAEDecode → Combine`\n   481→`TiledSampler → VAEDecodeTiled → Resize → Combine`\n   482→\n   483→Deterministic · High-res tiling · Dual-path blend · STG guidance\n   484→```\n   485→\n   486→Minimal markdown. No H2/H3. No bullet lists. Bold only the title. Code blocks only for flow chains. Inline list for capabilities. This is the least-bad version — always prefer panel rendering when available.\n   487→\n   488→---\n   489→\n   490→## 10. Design Checklist\n   491→\n   492→Before shipping any response, verify:\n   493→\n   494→- [ ] First visible line answers \"what is this\"\n   495→- [ ] Summary is ≤ 2 sentences\n   496→- [ ] No emoji anywhere\n   497→- [ ] Node references use slot colors\n   498→- [ ] Sections are collapsed by default (except flow + capabilities)\n   499→- [ ] No markdown headers (H2, H3, etc.)\n   500→- [ ] No bullet-point walls\n   501→- [ ] Action buttons present (what can the artist do next?)\n   502→- [ ] Response passes the 3-Second Test\n   503→- [ ] Text is terse, technical, active voice\n   504→- [ ] Nothing is restated or repeated\n   505→- [ ] Whitespace / spacing creates visual breathing room\n   506→\n   507→---\n   508→\n   509→*Design system v1.0 — ComfyUI Agent Panel*\n   510→*Colors sourced from docs.comfy.org/interface/appearance (dark theme)*\n   511→*Brand colors from ComfyUI identity: #00BB81 / #020410 / #FAFAFA*\n   512→"}]},"uuid":"7650d235-2ccb-48f6-8464-a529bd4d4582","timestamp":"2026-02-24T14:24:45.794Z"},"normalizedMessages":[],"type":"agent_progress","prompt":"Explore the comfyui-agent project at C:\\Users\\User\\comfyui-agent to find ALL UI-related code. Paragraphs with 8px margin-bottom (removed on last child)\n- **`.sd-code-inline`** — `code` text (11px mono, elevated bg, accent color, 1px 5px padding)\n- **`.sd-code-block`** — ````code blocks```` (11px mono, input bg, monospace, pre formatting, border)\n- **`.sd-text-bold`** — `**bold**` text (600 weight, primary color)\n\n#### 3.5 Typing Indicator (`.sd-typing`)\n- **Animation:** Three dots with staggered 0.15s/0.3s delays\n- **Keyframes:** Scale + opacity pulse (0.8→1.0, 0.3→1.0 opacity)\n- **Duration:** 1.2s infinite\n\n#### 3.6 Input Bar (`.sd-input-bar`)\n- **Layout:** Flex row with `gap: 6px`\n- **Input field:** 12px sans, border transitions to brand green on focus\n- **Send button:** Brand green background, dark text, 600 weight\n  - Hover: 0.9 opacity\n  - Active: 0.8 opacity\n  - Disabled: elevated bg, muted text\n- **Placeholder:** \"Ask me anything about your workflow...\"\n\n#### 3.7 Panel Components\nAll panels built dynamically via JavaScript factory functions in `chat.js`:\n\n**Panel structure:**\n```\n┌─────────────────────┐\n│ HEADER              │  Label + Badge, Title, Summary, Stats\n├─────────────────────┤\n│ SECTIONS (collapsed)│  Collapsible with colored dots\n│ ├─ Signal Flow      │  Flow chains showing data paths\n│ ├─ Models & Loaders │  Slot tags (colored chips)\n│ └─ ...              │\n├─────────────────────┤\n│ FOOTER              │  Status + Action buttons\n└─────────────────────┘\n```\n\n**Component Factories (in `chat.js`):**\n\n1. **`createSlotTag(label, slotType)`**\n   - Colored chip: dot (glow shadow at 55%) + label\n   - Background: `${color}1f` (12% opacity)\n   - Border: `${color}40` (25% opacity)\n   - Text color: slot color\n\n2. **`createCollapsibleSection(title, dotColor, count, defaultOpen, buildBody)`**\n   - Trigger button with colored dot + title + count + chevron\n   - Lazy body loading (only built on first expand)\n   - Chevron rotates 90° on open (CSS transition)\n   - `defaultOpen=false` except for Signal Flow and Capabilities\n\n3. **`createFlowChain(nodes, pathLabel)`**\n   - Nodes: `{label, slotType}` array\n   - Display: `Node1 → Node2 → Node3...`\n   - Node styling: slot color text + background at 10% opacity\n   - Arrow styling: muted color\n\n4. **`createDetailRow(label, value)`**\n   - Two-column layout with dotted leader\n   - Label: sans, 11px, muted\n   - Value: mono, 10.5px, secondary, right-aligned\n\n5. **`createCapabilityRow(label, desc, color)`**\n   - Dot (4px, colored) + label + separator line + description\n   - Label: sans, 11px, accent, 500 weight\n   - Description: sans, 10.5px, muted\n\n6. **`createActionFooter(status, actions)`**\n   - Status: mono, 10px, muted\n   - Buttons: secondary (transparent + border) or primary (brand green)\n   - Button padding: 5px 14px, border-radius: 4px\n\n7. **`createPanel(panelData)`**\n   - Master factory: builds header + sections + footer\n   - Schema:\n     ```javascript\n     {\n       type: \"workflow_analysis\"|\"discovery\"|\"execution\"|\"error\",\n       header: {\n         label, badge, title, summary,\n         stats: [{value, label}, ...]\n       },\n       sections: [{\n         title, dotColor, count, defaultOpen,\n         type: \"flow_chain\"|\"slot_tags\"|\"detail_rows\"|\"capability_rows\",\n         data: {...}\n       }],\n       footer: {status, actions: [{label, variant}]}\n     }\n     ```\n\n#### 3.8 Scrollbar Styling\n- **Width:** 3px\n- **Track:** Transparent\n- **Thumb:** `--sd-border` color\n- **Hover:** `--sd-text-muted` color\n\n---\n\n### 4. CHAT RENDERING (`chat.js`)\n\n**`renderText(rawText)` function:**\n- **Input:** Raw agent response text\n- **Parsing:** Splits on triple-backtick blocks\n- **Output:** DocumentFragment with styled elements (NO innerHTML)\n- **Supported markdown:**\n  - ````code blocks```` → `<pre class=\"sd-code-block\">`\n  - `` `inline code` `` → `<code class=\"sd-code-inline\">`\n  - `**bold**` → `<strong class=\"sd-text-bold\">`\n  - Paragraphs on double newlines\n  - Single newlines converted to `<br>` within paragraphs\n\n**Security note:** All content via `.textContent`, never `.innerHTML`\n\n---\n\n### 5. WEBSOCKET INTEGRATION (`sidebar.js`)\n\n**Connection:**\n- **URL:** `ws://localhost:8188/superduper/ws` (or `wss://` over HTTPS)\n- **Class:** `AgentConnection` with exponential backoff reconnection\n  - Initial delay: 1000ms\n  - Max delay: 15000ms\n  - Backoff multiplier: 1.5x\n\n**Message Types Received:**\n\n| Type | Payload | Handling |\n|------|---------|----------|\n| `connected` | `{conversation_id}` | Status update |\n| `text_delta` | `{text: string}` | Stream text chunk, show typing indicator |\n| `message` | `{content: string}` | Final rendered message |\n| `panel` | `{panel: object}` | Structured panel data |\n| `stage` | `{stage, detail}` | Update stage bar (UNDERSTAND/DISCOVER/PILOT/VERIFY/BRAIN/DONE) |\n| `error` | `{message, recoverable}` | Show error + potentially recover |\n\n**Workflow Injection:**\n- Captures current ComfyUI canvas workflow via `app.graphToPrompt()`\n- Sends in chat payload: `{type: \"chat\", content: string, workflow?: object}`\n- Backend uses for context injection into system prompt\n\n---\n\n### 6. BACKEND INTEGRATION (`routes.py`)\n\n**HTTP/WebSocket Routes:**\n\n```python\nPOST /superduper/chat      # Simple request/response (REST)\nGET  /superduper/status    # Agent + connection state\nGET  /superduper/ws        # WebSocket (primary interface)\n```\n\n**Panel Building Functions** (convert tool results to UI panels):\n\n1. **`_panel_workflow_analysis(result)`**\n   - Analyzes load_workflow / validate_workflow results\n   - Extracts: node count, connections, signal flow (via BFS)\n   - Groups nodes by slot type (MODEL, CLIP, VAE, etc.)\n   - Outputs: panel with Signal Flow (open) + collapsible slot sections\n\n2. **`_panel_discovery(result, tool_input)`**\n   - Converts discover tool results to discovery panels\n   - Shows: top 5 results with name, description, source, stats\n   - Tags: installed status + model type\n\n3. **`_classify_slot(class_type)`**\n   - Maps node class_type to slot type via keywords\n   - Used to assign colors to nodes\n\n**Stage Mapping** (tool name → intelligence layer):\n- UNDERSTAND: workflow parsing, node inspection, model listing\n- DISCOVER: search, discovery, templates\n- PILOT: workflow patching, node editing, session management\n- VERIFY: execution, validation, output analysis\n- BRAIN: vision, planning, memory, optimization, demo\n\n---\n\n### 7. COMFYUI EXTENSION REGISTRATION\n\n**File:** `ui/__init__.py`\n\n```python\nNODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS = {}\nNODE_CLASS_DISPLAY_MAPPINGS = {}\nWEB_DIRECTORY = \"./web\"\n```\n\n**Registration:**\n- Exports `WEB_DIRECTORY` → ComfyUI serves `/web/css` and `/web/js` as extension assets\n- Mounts aiohttp routes on ComfyUI's PromptServer at startup\n- Sidebar registers via `app.extensionManager.registerSidebarTab()`\n\n**Sidebar Configuration:**\n```javascript\n{\n  id: \"superduper\",\n  icon: \"pi pi-bolt\",              // Primeicons icon\n  title: \"SUPER DUPER\",\n  tooltip: \"SUPER DUPER AI Co-pilot\",\n  type: \"custom\",\n  render(el) { buildSidebar(el); }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 8. DESIGN PHILOSOPHY (from Design System Document)\n\n**Three Core Principles:**\n\n1. **Summary-First** — First visible element answers \"what is this\" in one line\n2. **Progressive Disclosure** — Show minimum, let user drill; collapse by default\n3. **Native Vocabulary** — Use ComfyUI's colors, terms, spatial patterns (no emoji, no markdown headers)\n\n**The 3-Second Test:** Artist must understand what happened, what matters, and what to do next within 3 seconds of looking at a panel.\n\n**Semantic Color Mapping:**\n- Use slot colors when displaying node types or concepts\n- E.g., \"loaded a model\" → MODEL color (#B39DDB)\n- Flow chains show signal path through nodes with colored nodes\n\n---\n\n### 9. TYPOGRAPHY SYSTEM\n\n**Type Scale:**\n\n| Element | Size | Font | Weight | Tracking |\n|---------|------|------|--------|----------|\n| Panel title | 16px | sans | 600 | -0.02em |\n| Section header | 12px | sans | 500 | 0.01em |\n| Body text | 12px | sans | 400 | normal |\n| Label (uppercase) | 10px | mono | 400 | 0.06-0.08em |\n| Tag / code | 11px | mono | 400 | 0.01em |\n| Detail value | 10.5px | mono | 400 | 0.01em |\n| Stat number | 14px | mono | 600 | normal |\n\n**Rules:**\n- Mono for: node names, values, counts, code, tags, status labels\n- Sans for: descriptions, titles, body text, buttons\n- Never mix mono and sans on the same line (except label + value pairs)\n\n---\n\n### 10. SPACING & LAYOUT\n\n**Grid Unit:** 4px base\n\n| Element | Value | Purpose |\n|---------|-------|---------|\n| Micro gap | 4px | Between dot and label |\n| Small gap | 6px | Between tags in row |\n| Medium gap | 8-10px | Between items in list |\n| Section padding | 14-16px | Horizontal |\n| Section gap | 12px | Between content blocks |\n| Header padding | 16px top, 14px bottom | |\n| Footer padding | 10px vertical | |\n\n**Vertical Rhythm:**\n- 1px separator line between sections (`--sd-border-subtle`)\n- 12px gap between content blocks within sections\n- No double-lines or heavy borders — separation via color shifts + spacing\n\n---\n\n### 11. ANTI-PATTERNS (Never Do This)\n\n| Anti-Pattern | Why It Fails | Solution |\n|--------------|-------------|----------|\n| Emoji as headers 🎯 | Not native to ComfyUI | Colored dots using slot colors |\n| Markdown bullet walls | Equal visual weight | Collapsible sections + tags |\n| \"What This Excels At\" | Sales copy | \"Capabilities\" spec sheet (concrete) |\n| Bold **every** node name | Visual noise | Colored mono-font tags |\n| \"Would you like me to...\" | Passive, wastes space | Action buttons in footer |\n| Restating the question | Artist knows their ask | Jump to answer |\n| H2/H3 markdown headers | Flat hierarchy | Progressive disclosure (collapse) |\n| \"This is sophisticated\" | Subjective filler | Concrete details: \"Dual-path with tiling\" |\n| Long paragraphs | Nobody reads para 3 | Summary + collapsible detail |\n| Numbered lists for features | Implies sequence | Capability rows or inline |\n\n---\n\n### 12. CHAT RENDERING (`chat.js`)\n\n**`renderText(rawText)` function:**\n- **Input:** Raw agent response text\n- **Parsing:** Splits on triple-backtick blocks\n- **Output:** DocumentFragment with styled elements (NO innerHTML)\n- **Supported markdown:**\n  - ````code blocks```` → `<pre class=\"sd-code-block\">`\n  - `` `inline code` `` → `<code class=\"sd-code-inline\">`\n  - `**bold**` → `<strong class=\"sd-text-bold\">`\n  - Paragraphs on double newlines\n  - Single newlines converted to `<br>` within paragraphs\n\n**Security note:** All content via `.textContent`, never `.innerHTML`\n\n---\n\n### 5. WEBSOCKET INTEGRATION (`sidebar.js`)\n\n**Connection:**\n- **URL:** `ws://localhost:8188/superduper/ws` (or `wss://` over HTTPS)\n- **Class:** `AgentConnection` with exponential backoff reconnection\n  - Initial delay: 1000ms\n  - Max delay: 15000ms\n  - Backoff multiplier: 1.5x\n\n**Message Types Received:**\n\n| Type | Payload | Handling |\n|------|---------|----------|\n| `connected` | `{conversation_id}` | Status update |\n| `text_delta` | `{text: string}` | Stream text chunk, show typing indicator |\n| `message` | `{content: string}` | Final rendered message |\n| `panel` | `{panel: object}` | Structured panel data |\n| `stage` | `{stage, detail}` | Update stage bar (UNDERSTAND/DISCOVER/PILOT/VERIFY/BRAIN/DONE) |\n| `error` | `{message, recoverable}` | Show error + potentially recover |\n\n**Workflow Injection:**\n- Captures current ComfyUI canvas workflow via `app.graphToPrompt()`\n- Sends in chat payload: `{type: \"chat\", content: string, workflow?: object}`\n- Backend uses for context injection into system prompt\n\n---\n\n### 6. BACKEND INTEGRATION (`routes.py`)\n\n**HTTP/WebSocket Routes:**\n\n```python\nPOST /superduper/chat      # Simple request/response (REST)\nGET  /superduper/status    # Agent + connection state\nGET  /superduper/ws        # WebSocket (primary interface)\n```\n\n**Panel Building Functions** (convert tool results to UI panels):\n\n1. **`_panel_workflow_analysis(result)`**\n   - Analyzes load_workflow / validate_workflow results\n   - Extracts: node count, connections, signal flow (via BFS)\n   - Groups nodes by slot type (MODEL, CLIP, VAE, etc.)\n   - Outputs: panel with Signal Flow (open) + collapsible slot sections\n\n2. **`_panel_discovery(result, tool_input)`**\n   - Converts discover tool results to discovery panels\n   - Shows: top 5 results with name, description, source, stats\n   - Tags: installed status + model type\n\n3. **`_classify_slot(class_type)`**\n   - Maps node class_type to slot type via keywords\n   - Used to assign colors to nodes\n\n**Stage Mapping** (tool name → intelligence layer):\n- UNDERSTAND: workflow parsing, node inspection, model listing\n- DISCOVER: search, discovery, templates\n- PILOT: workflow patching, node editing, session management\n- VERIFY: execution, validation, output analysis\n- BRAIN: vision, planning, memory, optimization, demo\n\n---\n\n### 7. COMFYUI EXTENSION REGISTRATION\n\n**File:** `ui/__init__.py`\n\n```python\nNODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS = {}\nNODE_CLASS_DISPLAY_MAPPINGS = {}\nWEB_DIRECTORY = \"./web\"\n```\n\n**Registration:**\n- Exports `WEB_DIRECTORY` → ComfyUI serves `/web/css` and `/web/js` as extension assets\n- Mounts aiohttp routes on ComfyUI's PromptServer at startup\n- Sidebar registers via `app.extensionManager.registerSidebarTab()`\n\n**Sidebar Configuration:**\n```javascript\n{\n  id: \"superduper\",\n  icon: \"pi pi-bolt\",              // Primeicons icon\n  title: \"SUPER DUPER\",\n  tooltip: \"SUPER DUPER AI Co-pilot\",\n  type: \"custom\",\n  render(el) { buildSidebar(el); }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 8. DESIGN PHILOSOPHY (from Design System Document)\n\n**Three Core Principles:**\n\n1. **Summary-First** — First visible element answers \"what is this\" in one line\n2. **Progressive Disclosure** — Show minimum, let user drill; collapse by default\n3. **Native Vocabulary** — Use ComfyUI's colors, terms, spatial patterns (no emoji, no markdown headers)\n\n**The 3-Second Test:** Artist must understand what happened, what matters, and what to do next within 3 seconds of looking at a panel.\n\n**Semantic Color Mapping:**\n- Use slot colors when displaying node types or concepts\n- E.g., \"loaded a model\" → MODEL color (#B39DDB)\n- Flow chains show signal path through nodes with colored nodes\n\n---\n\n### 9. TYPOGRAPHY SYSTEM\n\n**Type Scale:**\n\n| Element | Size | Font | Weight | Tracking |\n|---------|------|------|--------|----------|\n| Panel title | 16px | sans | 600 | -0.02em |\n| Section header | 12px | sans | 500 | 0.01em |\n| Body text | 12px | sans | 400 | normal |\n| Label (uppercase) | 10px | mono | 400 | 0.06-0.08em |\n| Tag / code | 11px | mono | 400 | 0.01em |\n| Detail value | 10.5px | mono | 400 | 0.01em |\n| Stat number | 14px | mono | 600 | normal |\n\n**Rules:**\n- Mono for: node names, values, counts, code, tags, status labels\n- Sans for: descriptions, titles, body text, buttons\n- Never mix mono and sans on the same line (except label + value pairs)\n\n---\n\n### 10. SPACING & LAYOUT\n\n**Grid Unit:** 4px base\n\n| Element | Value | Purpose |\n|---------|-------|---------|\n| Micro gap | 4px | Between dot and label |\n| Small gap | 6px | Between tags in row |\n| Medium gap | 8-10px | Between items in list |\n| Section padding | 14-16px | Horizontal |\n| Section gap | 12px | Between content blocks |\n| Header padding | 16px top, 14px bottom | |\n| Footer padding | 10px vertical | |\n\n**Vertical Rhythm:**\n- 1px separator line between sections (`--sd-border-subtle`)\n- 12px gap between content blocks within sections\n- No double-lines or heavy borders — separation via color shifts + spacing\n\n---\n\n### 11. ANTI-PATTERNS (Never Do This)\n\n| Anti-Pattern | Why It Fails | Solution |\n|--------------|-------------|----------|\n| Emoji as headers 🎯 | Not native to ComfyUI | Colored dots using slot colors |\n| Markdown bullet walls | Equal visual weight | Collapsible sections + tags |\n| \"What This Excels At\" | Sales copy | \"Capabilities\" spec sheet (concrete) |\n| Bold **every** node name | Visual noise | Colored mono-font tags |\n| \"Would you like me to...\" | Passive, wastes space | Action buttons in footer |\n| Restating the question | Artist knows their ask | Jump to answer |\n| H2/H3 markdown headers | Flat hierarchy | Progressive disclosure (collapse) |\n| \"This is sophisticated\" | Subjective filler | Concrete details: \"Dual-path with tiling\" |\n| Long paragraphs | Nobody reads para 3 | Summary + collapsible detail |\n| Numbered lists for features | Implies sequence | Capability rows or inline |\n\n---\n\n### 12. It must be printed after every task completion — no exceptions.\n     5→\n     6→## Symbol Legend\n     7→\n     8→| Symbol | Meaning | When to Use |\n     9→|--------|---------|-------------|\n    10→| ✓ | Done | Task completed successfully |\n    11→| ▶ | Active | Task currently being worked on |\n    12→| ○ | Pending | Task not yet started |\n    13→| ✗ | Failed | Task failed (include error before status bar) |\n    14→| █ | Progress fill | Filled portion of progress bar |\n    15→| ░ | Progress empty | Unfilled portion of progress bar |\n    16→\n    17→## Progress Bar Calculation\n    18→\n    19→```\n    20→total_tasks = sum of all tasks across all agents\n    21→done_tasks = count of ✓ tasks\n    22→active_tasks = count of ▶ tasks\n    23→pct = round((done_tasks / total_tasks) * 100)\n    24→\n    25→bar_width = 20 characters\n    26→filled = round(pct / 100 * bar_width)\n    27→empty = bar_width - It must be printed after every task completion — no exceptions.\n\n## Symbol Legend\n\n| Symbol | Meaning | When to Use |\n|--------|---------|-------------|\n| ✓ | Done | Task completed successfully |\n| ▶ | Active | Task currently being worked on |\n| ○ | Pending | Task not yet started |\n| ✗ | Failed | Task failed (include error before status bar) |\n| █ | Progress fill | Filled portion of progress bar |\n| ░ | Progress empty | Unfilled portion of progress bar |\n\n## Progress Bar Calculation\n\n```\ntotal_tasks = sum of all tasks across all agents\ndone_tasks = count of ✓ tasks\nactive_tasks = count of ▶ tasks\npct = round((done_tasks / total_tasks) * 100)\n\nbar_width = 20 characters\nfilled = round(pct / 100 * bar_width)\nempty = bar_width - filled\nbar = \"█\" * filled + \"░\" * empty\n```\n\nPer-phase calculation is the same but scoped to tasks within that phase.\n\n## Format\n\n```\n╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n║  {PROJECT} — {SPRINT} STATUS                                ║\n╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n║                                                              ║\n║  Phase {N}: {NAME}          [{bar}] {pct}%                  ║\n║    {AGENT}  {icon} {T1} {sym}  {T2} {sym}  ...              ║\n║                                                              ║\n║  Overall: [{bar}] {pct}%  ({done}/{total} tasks)            ║\n║                                                              ║\n║  Legend: ✓ done  ▶ active  ○ pending  ✗ failed              ║\n╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n```\n\n## Rules\n\n1. **Print after EVERY task** — not just phases or milestones\n2. **Show ALL phases** — including future ones (they show all ○)\n3. **On failure** — print error details ABOVE the status bar, then the bar with ✗\n4. **On gate check** — print the bar showing gate result\n5. **Final bar** — on sprint completion, show the complete bar with summary stats\n\n## Scaling\n\nFor sprints with many tasks (30+), abbreviate:\n\n```\n║    AGENT  ◆ [8/10 ✓, 1 ▶, 1 ○]                             ║\n```\n\nFor sprints with few tasks (<10), show every task ID individually.\n\n## Agent Icons\n\nAssign each agent a unique Unicode symbol for visual scanning:\n\n```\n◆ ⟡ ◈ ⬡ ◇ ○ ◉ ▣ △ ☆\n```\n\nPick icons that are visually distinct from each other. Consistency matters — once assigned, an agent keeps its icon for the entire sprint.\n","numLines":76,"startLine":1,"totalLines":76}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ccd241b7-af31-4988-a1c6-1995d4abf691"}

dependencies | Migration | Map before you move |\n   285→| Custom | Start from DISPATCH_TEMPLATE.md | Build your own |\n   286→\n   287→## Sizing Guide\n   288→\n   289→| Sprint Size | Agents | Phases 293→| Large | 5-7 | 3-4 | 20-35 | 1-2 hours |\n   294→| XL | 6+ | 4+ | 35+ | 2+ hours (consider splitting) |\n   295→\n   296→If you're over 35 tasks, consider splitting into two sequential sprints.\n Status bar — print after EVERY task\n```\n\n---\n\n## Pattern 2: Refactor Sprint (4 agents, 3 phases)\n\nUse for: Restructuring existing code without changing behavior.\nAgents: Analyst, Mover, ANALYZE (sequential — one agent)\n\n### Agent ANALYST — Code Archaeology Expert\nTask A1: Map all cross-file dependencies for {target_module}\nTask A2: Generate move plan with old_path → new_path table\n\n### PHASE 1 GATE\n```bash\ntest -f docs/{analysis}.md && echo \"Analysis: OK\"\n```\n\n## PHASE 2: RESTRUCTURE (parallel)\n\n### Agent MOVER — File Structure Expert\nTask M1: Create new directory structure\nTask M2: Move files per the move plan\nTask M3: Update __init__.py exports\n\n### Agent UPDATE — Import/Reference Expert\nTask U1: Update all imports referencing moved files\nTask U2: Update config files / registries\nTask U3: Update documentation paths\n\n### PHASE 2 GATE\n```bash\npython -c \"import {package}; print('Package: OK')\"\npytest tests/ -q --tb=short\n```\n\n## PHASE 3: VERIFY (sequential)\n\n### Agent VERIFY — Regression Expert\nTask V1: Run full test suite, report any failures\nTask V2: Verify no circular imports\nTask V3: Verify no dead imports (unused)\n\n### PHASE 3 GATE (FINAL)\n```bash\npytest tests/ -q --tb=short\npython -c \"import {package}; print('Clean import: OK')\"\n# Verify no test was accidentally deleted\ntest $(find tests/ -name \"test_*.py\" | wc -l) -ge {expected_test_count}\n```\n```\n\n---\n\n## Pattern 3: Knowledge Expansion (2 agents, 2 phases)\n\nUse for: Adding RAG entries, documentation, or knowledge base cause analysis\nPhase 2: FIX + VALIDATE — Surgeon fixes (parallel with) Validator writing tests\n\n---\n\n## Pattern 5: Migration Sprint (4 agents, 3 phases)\n\nUse for: Migrating from one system/API/framework to another.\nAgents: Mapper, Builder, Migrator, Validator.\n\nPhase 1: MAP — Mapper analyzes old + new APIs, builds translation table\nPhase 2: BUILD — Builder creates new implementation, Migrator updates callsites\nPhase 3: VALIDATE — Validator runs comparative tests old vs new\n\n---\n\n## Choosing Your Pattern\n\n| Situation | Pattern | Why |\n|-----------|---------|-----|\n| Adding a feature | Feature Build | Clean backend/frontend/test split |\n| Restructuring code | Refactor | Analyze-first prevents breakage |\n| Adding docs/RAG | Knowledge | Research then integrate |\n| Fixing bugs | Bug Fix | Diagnose before cutting |\n| Swapping dependencies | Migration | Map before you move |\n| Custom | Start from DISPATCH_TEMPLATE.md | Build your own |\n\n## Sizing Guide\n\n| Sprint Size | Agents | Phases | Tasks | Estimated Time |\n|-------------|--------|--------|-------|----------------|\n| Small | 2-3 | 2 | 5-10 | 15-30 min |\n| Medium | 3-5 | 2-3 | 10-20 | 30-60 min |\n| Large | 5-7 | 3-4 | 20-35 | 1-2 hours |\n| XL | 6+ | 4+ | 35+ | 2+ hours (consider splitting) |\n\nIf you're over 35 tasks, consider splitting into two _build_system(self):\n    76→        from agent.system_prompt import build_system_prompt\n    77→        base = build_system_prompt()\n    78→\n    79→        # Append workflow context if available\n    80→        extras = []\n    81→        if self.workflow_summary:\n    82→            s = self.workflow_summary\n    83→            extras.append(\n    84→                \"\\n\\n## Current Workflow 103→_conversations: dict[str, ConversationState] = {}\n   104→\n   105→\n   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   107→# Workflow injection (sidebar -> agent state)\n   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   109→\n   110→def _inject_workflow(conv: ConversationState, workflow_data: dict) -> None:\n   111→    \"\"\"Inject live workflow data into agent state and conversation context.\n   112→\n   113→    Skips reload if the workflow hasn't changed (preserves undo history).\n   114→    Runs find_missing_nodes on first injection (best-effort).\n   115→    \"\"\"\n   116→    # Quick change detection via hash of sorted JSON\n   117→    import hashlib\n   118→    from agent.tools._util import to_json as _to_json\n   119→    wf_hash = hash(_to_json(workflow_data))\n   120→\n   121→    if wf_hash == conv._workflow_hash:\n   122→        # Workflow unchanged — rebuild system prompt (keeps context fresh)\n   123→        # but don't reload (preserves undo history)\n   124→        conv._build_system()\n   125→        return\n   126→\n   127→    conv._workflow_hash = wf_hash\n   128→\n   129→    # Load into PILOT engine\n   130→    from agent.tools.workflow_patch import load_workflow_from_data\n   131→    err = load_workflow_from_data(workflow_data, source=\"<sidebar>\")\n   132→    if err:\n   133→        log.warning(\"Workflow injection failed: %s\", err)\n   134→        return\n   135→\n   136→    # Summarize for system prompt\n   137→    from 152→                conv.missing_nodes = None\n   153→        except Exception as e:\n   154→            log.debug(\"Missing nodes check skipped: %s\", e)\n   155→\n   156→    # Force system prompt (tool result -> structured panel data for frontend)\n   162→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   163→\n   164→# Ordered slot classification rules (checked first to last; first match wins).\n   165→# More specific patterns before generic ones to avoid false matches.\n   166→_SLOT_RULES = [\n   167→    (\"controlnet\", [\"controlnet\", \"control_net\", \"preprocessor\", \"canny\", \"openpose\"]),\n   168→    (\"vae\",        [\"vae\"]),\n   169→    (\"clip\",       [\"clip\", \"textencode\", \"t5\"]),\n   170→    (\"latent\",     [\"latent\", \"preview\", \"save\", \"video\", \"combine\"]),\n   178→    (\"model\",      [\"checkpoint\", \"model\", \"unet\", \"lora\", \"loader\"]),\n   179→]\n   180→\n   181→\n   182→def _classify_slot(class_type: str) -> str:\n   183→    \"\"\"Keyword-based class_type -> slot type string.\"\"\"\n   184→    ct_lower = class_type.lower()\n   185→    for slot_type, keywords in _SLOT_RULES:\n   186→        for kw in keywords:\n   187→            if kw in ct_lower:\n   188→                return slot_type\n   189→    return \"model\"  # fallback\n   190→\n   191→\n   192→def _extract_flow_chain(nodes: dict, max_nodes: int = 8) -> list[dict]:\n   193→    \"\"\"BFS from root nodes to build a signal flow chain.\n   194→\n   195→    Args:\n   196→        nodes: API format {node_id: {class_type, inputs}}\n   197→        max_nodes: Maximum nodes in chain\n   198→\n   199→    Returns:\n   200→        List of {label, slotType} dicts\n   201→    \"\"\"\n   202→    if not nodes:\n   203→        return []\n   204→\n   205→    # Find nodes that receive input from other nodes (have upstream connections)\n   206→    has_upstream = set()\n   207→    for nid, ndata in nodes.items():\n   208→        for val in (ndata.get(\"inputs\") or {}).values():\n   209→            if isinstance(val, list) and len(val) == 2:\n   210→                # This node (nid) receives input from val[0]\n   211→                has_upstream.add(nid)\n   212→\n   213→    # Roots: nodes with no upstream connections (source nodes)\n   214→    all_ids = set(nodes.keys())\n   215→    roots = all_ids - has_upstream\n   216→    if not roots:\n   217→        roots = {sorted(all_ids)[0]} if all_ids else set()\n   218→\n   219→    # BFS from roots\n   220→    visited = []\n   221→    queue_bfs = list(sorted(roots))\n   222→    seen = set()\n   223→\n   224→    while queue_bfs and len(visited) < max_nodes:\n   225→        nid = queue_bfs.pop(0)\n   226→        if nid in seen:\n   227→            continue\n   228→        seen.add(nid)\n   229→\n   230→        ndata = nodes.get(nid)\n   231→        if not ndata:\n   232→            continue\n   233→\n   234→        ct = ndata.get(\"class_type\", \"?\")\n   235→        visited.append({\"label\": ct, \"slotType\": _classify_slot(ct)})\n   236→\n   237→        # Find downstream nodes (nodes that reference this nid)\n   238→        for other_id, other_data in nodes.items():\n   239→            if other_id in seen:\n   240→                continue\n   241→            for val in (other_data.get(\"inputs\") or {}).values():\n   242→                if isinstance(val, list) and len(val) == 2 and str(val[0]) == nid:\n   243→                    queue_bfs.append(other_id)\n   244→                    break\n   245→\n   246→    return visited\n   247→\n   248→\n   249→# Slot type -> hex color for panel dot colors\n   250→_SLOT_HEX = {\n   251→    \"clip\": \"#FFD500\", \"clip_vision\": \"#A8DADC\", \"conditioning\": \"#FFA931\",\n   252→    \"controlnet\": \"#6EE7B7\", \"image\": \"#64B5F6\", \"latent\": \"#FF9CF9\",\n   253→    \"mask\": \"#81C784\", \"model\": \"#B39DDB\", \"style_model\": \"#C2FFAE\",\n   254→    \"vae\": \"#FF6E6E\", \"noise\": \"#B0B0B0\", \"guider\": \"#66FFFF\",\n   255→    \"sampler\": \"#ECB4B4\", \"sigmas\": \"#CDFFCD\",\n   256→}\n   257→\n   258→\n   259→def _panel_workflow_analysis(result: dict) -> dict | None:\n   260→    \"\"\"Build panel data from load_workflow / validate_workflow result.\"\"\"\n   261→    # Extract nodes from result\n   262→    nodes 267→    if not nodes and \"node_count\" in result:\n   268→        # Minimal header-only panel\n   269→        return {\n   270→            \"type\": \"workflow_analysis\",\n   271→            \"header\": {\n   272→                \"label\": \"workflow \\u00b7 analysis\",\n   273→                \"badge\": result.get(\"format\", \"workflow\"),\n   274→                \"title\":    f\"{result.get('connection_count', '?')} connections. \"\n   277→                           f\"Format: {result.get('format', 'unknown')}.\",\n   278→                \"stats\": [\n   279→ slot = _classify_slot(ct)\n   296→        groups.setdefault(slot, []).append({\"label\": ct, \"slotType\": slot})\n   297→\n   298→    # Count connections\n   299→    conn_count = 0\n   300→    for 333→    # Node group sections (collapsed)\n   334→    # Priority order for display\n   335→    group_order = [\"model\", \"clip\", \"conditioning\", \"sampler\", \"vae\",\n   336→                   \"controlnet\", \"image\", \"latent\", \"mask\", \"noise\",\n   337→                   \"guider\", \"sigmas\"]\n   338→    group_labels = {\n   339→        \"model\": \"Models & Loaders\", \"clip\":      \"latent\": \"Latent Space\", \"mask\": \"Masks\", \"noise\": \"Noise\",\n   343→        \"guider\": \"Guidance\", \"sigmas\": \"Noise Schedule\",\n   344→    }\n   345→\n   346→    for slot in             {\"value\": wf_type, \"label\": \"type\"},\n   389→            ],\n   390→        },\n   391→        \"sections\": sections,\n   392→        \"footer\": {\"status\": \"loaded\", \"actions\": [\n   393→            {\"label\": \"Modify\", \"variant\": \"secondary\"},\n   394→            {\"label\": \"Run\", \"variant\": \"primary\"},\n   395→        ]},\n   396→    }\n   397→\n   398→\n   399→def _panel_discovery(result: dict, tool_input: dict) -> dict | None:\n   400→    \"\"\"Build panel data from discover tool results.\"\"\"\n   401→    results_list = Returns panel dict or None.\"\"\"\n   479→    if name not in _PANEL_TOOLS:\n   480→        return None\n   481→\n   482→    try:\n   483→        result = json.loads(result_json) if ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   499→# Synchronous agent runner (called from thread)\n   500→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   501→\n   502→def _run_agent_sync(conv: ConversationState, user_text: str, msg_queue: queue.Queue):\n   503→    \"\"\"Run the agent loop synchronously, pushing events to msg_queue.\n   504→\n   505→    Events are dicts: {\"type\": \"text_delta\"|\"tool_call\"|\"stage\"|\"done\"|\"error\", ...}\n   506→    \"\"\"\n   507→    from agent.main import run_agent_turn\n   508→    from agent.tools import ALL_TOOLS\n   509→\n   510→    conv._build_system()\n   511→    conv.messages.append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user_text})\n   512→\n   513→    max_turns = 15  # safety limit per user message\n   514→\n   515→    for turn in range(max_turns):\n   516→        try:\n   517→            def on_text(text):\n   518→                msg_queue.put({\"type\": \"text_delta\", \"text\": text})\n   519→\n   520→            def on_tool(name, inp):\n   521→                # Infer stage from tool name\n   522→ \"stage\": stage,\n   527→                })\n   528→\n   529→            def on_result(name, inp, result_json):\n   530→                panel = _build_panel_for_tool(name, inp, result_json)\n   531→                if panel:\n   532→                    msg_queue.put({\"type\": \"panel\", \"panel\": panel})\n   533→\n   534→            conv.messages, done = run_agent_turn(\n   535→                _client,\n   536→                conv.messages,\n   537→                conv.system_prompt,\n   538→                on_text_delta=on_text,\n   539→                on_tool_call=on_tool,\n   540→                on_tool_result=on_result,\n   541→            )\n   542→\n   543→            if done:\n   544→                msg_queue.put({\"type\": \"done\"})\n   545→                return\n   546→\n   547→        except Exception as e:\n   548→            log.error(\"Agent turn error: %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n   549→            msg_queue.put({\"type\": \"error\", \"message\": str(e)})\n   550→            return\n   551→\n   552→    msg_queue.put({\"type\": \"done\"})\n   553→\n   554→\n   555→def _infer_stage(tool_name: str) -> str:\n   556→    \"\"\"Map tool name to intelligence layer \"read_node_source\",\n   563→    }\n   564→    discover = {\n   565→        \"discover\", \"find_missing_nodes\", \"check_registry_freshness\",\n   566→        \"get_install_instructions\",     \"get_workflow_diff\", \"save_workflow\", \"reset_workflow\",\n   574→        \"add_node\", \"connect_nodes\", \"set_input\",\n   575→        \"save_session\", \"load_session\", \"list_sessions\",   \"get_output_path\", \"verify_execution\",\n   581→    }\n   582→\n   583→    if tool_name in understand:\n   584→        return \"UNDERSTAND\"\n   585→    elif tool_name in discover:\n   586→ return \"BRAIN\"\n   593→\n   594→\n   595→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   596→# WebSocket handler\n   597→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   598→\n   599→async def websocket_handler(request):\n   600→    \"\"\"Bidirectional WebSocket for real-time sidebar <->
